# TXC — thaitax_schema_dump_reconciliation — Restricted Technical Evidence

> **RESTRICTED — TECHNICAL EVIDENCE — NOT FOR NEUTRAL DISTRIBUTION**
> Status: **DEEPSEEK-REPORTED / PENDING CLAUDE VERIFICATION**
> Unit: TXC `thaitax_schema_dump_reconciliation` (Thai Tax Core lane, topic 11: source-to-schema and source-to-dump reconciliation; produces the **Field-to-Schema Mapping**) · Source revision: `19.0.post20260921` (Odoo 19 Community only) · Date: 2026-10-02
> Method: AST scan of Python model declarations in all 356 installed Community modules (fields, `_inherit`/`_name`/`_inherits` closure, `models.Constraint/Index/UniqueIndex`), compared field-by-field with the restored database (`information_schema`, `pg_constraint`, `pg_indexes`, `ir_model_fields`, `ir_model_constraint`) for 44 tax-path models; seeded Thai configuration compared with `l10n_th` seed files. Read-only; no business values queried; no Odoo started; nothing from Extra/Custom/OEEL/OPL/Enterprise code was opened. Anything needing execution is flagged `RT`.
> Prior work reused: B01 (`03_DB_RECONCILIATION/B01_SOURCE_DB_RECONCILIATION_AND_L0_CONFIG_CONTEXT.md` and `B01_PER_MODULE_SOURCE_DB_COUNTS_356.tsv`), U13 (tax/chart), U24 (Thai localisation), U11/U12 (entries/payments). No V-level, completeness, coverage % or gate pass is asserted. Claim IDs `VDR-TXC-C###`; neutral IDs `N-TXC-###` in `02_NEUTRAL_KNOWLEDGE/TXC_thaitax_schema_dump_reconciliation_NEUTRAL.md`.
> Scripts and the full row-level working data are kept in the restricted local area `~/STATE03_RESTRICTED_LOCAL/vdr/txc/` (not part of the output set).

## 0. Scope, vocabulary and result summary

**Models covered (44):** CORE (full field mapping): `account.tax`, `account.tax.group`, `account.tax.repartition.line`, `account.account.tag`, `account.report`, `account.report.line`, `account.report.expression`, `account.report.column`, `account.report.external.value`, `account.fiscal.position`, `account.fiscal.position.account`, `account.move`, `account.move.line`, `account.partial.reconcile`, `account.full.reconcile`, `account.account`, `account.code.mapping`, `account.journal`, `account.payment`, `account.lock_exception`, `account.cash.rounding`, `account.reconcile.model`, `account.reconcile.model.line`, `account.chart.template`, `res.partner.bank`, `account.setup.bank.manual.config`. PERIPHERAL (tax/currency/fiscal/lock/Thai-relevant fields mapped; every field still reconciled): `res.partner`, `res.users`, `res.company`, `product.template`, `product.product`, `product.category`, `sale.order`, `sale.order.line`, `purchase.order`, `purchase.order.line`, `stock.move`, `res.currency`, `res.currency.rate`, `res.country`, `res.country.group`, `res.country.state`, `hr.expense`, `account.invoice.report`.

**Classification vocabulary (mapping `Class` column):** `STORED` (own column, or attachment-backed binary) · `NON-STORED COMPUTED` (compute without `store`, no column) · `RELATED` (related field; `Stored?` says whether a column exists) · `COMPANY-DEPENDENT` (jsonb per-company map in the model's own column) · `TRANSLATABLE` (jsonb per-language column) · `RELATION TABLE` (stored many2many through a link table) · `O2M INVERSE (non-stored)` (extension of the vocabulary: one2many has no column; the FK sits on the comodel) · `DB-ONLY` (present in DB, no source declaration: here only ORM-implicit fields) · `SOURCE-ONLY` (declared in source, absent from DB: none among installed modules; listed for uninstalled modules in CAP-TXC-10). Delegated (`_inherits`) fields carry the parent declaration pointer and are noted as such.

**Filter rule for PERIPHERAL models:** the mapping lists fields whose name matches tax/fiscal/vat/currency/lock/branch/rounding/account/journal/country/price/amount/property-account patterns, whose comodel is tax/fiscal-position/account/currency/tag/journal/move/payment/country, monetary fields, and anything declared by `l10n_th`; `res.users` lists only ORM-implicit and `l10n_th` rows; bank-account models list proxy/country/currency/account-number rows. Reconciliation (SOURCE-ONLY/DB-ONLY/type/store/company-dependent/translate checks) was run over **all** registered fields of every model, mapped or not.

| Fields registered in DB for these models (ir_model_fields) | 3183 |

| SOURCE-ONLY fields among installed modules | 0 |
| DB-ONLY fields without any explanation | 0 |
| DB columns without an ir_model_fields row | 0 |
| Stored fields lacking a column (excluding o2m and attachment binaries) | 0 |
| Type / store / company-dependent / translate / required / index mismatches (source effective vs DB) | 0 |
| Rows in the Field-to-Schema Mapping register | 1285 (1490 field rows; ORM-implicit fields combined to one row per model) |
| Class counts across all registered fields | COMPANY-DEPENDENT 45, DB-ONLY 249, NON-STORED COMPUTED 844, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 201, RELATED 644, RELATION TABLE 82, STORED 1057, TRANSLATABLE 61 |
| Declared SQL objects for these models present in DB | 56 of 56 |
| Many-to-many link tables reconciled | 67 |
| Uninstalled tax-path modules inventoried (SOURCE-ONLY) | 4 modules with field extensions + wizard model; 22 field declarations |

## CAP-TXC-01 Tax definition and classification data

**Function-ID(s):** FUNCTION MAPPING REQUIRED

### D1 Business purpose
Hold the catalogue of tax definitions (VAT and withholding in the Thai set), their grouping, the distribution of base and tax amounts to accounts, and the reporting tags that feed returns. VDR-TXC-C046 VDR-TXC-C048 VDR-TXC-C054 VDR-TXC-C062

### D2 Architecture / data relationships
- `account.tax` (27 columns of 63 fields; table `account_tax`): `tax_group_id` -> `account.tax.group` (FK->account_tax_group ON DELETE RESTRICT); `country_id`, `company_id`; `invoice_repartition_line_ids`/`refund_repartition_line_ids`/`repartition_line_ids` are one2many through `account.tax.repartition.line.tax_id` (FK->account_tax ON DELETE CASCADE). VDR-TXC-C052 VDR-TXC-C053
- Distribution line (`account_tax_repartition_line`): `repartition_type` (base/tax), `document_type` (invoice/refund), `factor_percent` stored (`factor` derived), `account_id` (FK->account_account ON DELETE SET NULL), `use_in_tax_closing`, tags via `account_account_tag_account_tax_repartition_line_rel(account_tax_repartition_line_id,account_account_tag_id)`. VDR-TXC-C082 VDR-TXC-C085 VDR-TXC-C086
- Links among taxes: children `account_tax_filiation_rel(parent_tax,child_tax)`, original/replacing `account_tax_alternatives(dest_tax_id,src_tax_id)` / `account_tax_alternatives(src_tax_id,dest_tax_id)`, fiscal positions `account_fiscal_position_account_tax_rel(account_tax_id,account_fiscal_position_id)`. VDR-TXC-C061 VDR-TXC-C065 VDR-TXC-C066 VDR-TXC-C064
- Tags (`account_account_tag`): `applicability` (taxes/accounts), `country_id` (FK->res_country ON DELETE SET NULL), translatable `name`; `balance_negate` and `report_expression_id` are non-stored computed, resolved by a SQL join in `_field_to_sql`. VDR-TXC-C089 VDR-TXC-C092
- Translatable jsonb columns: tax `name`, `description`, `invoice_label`, `invoice_legal_notes`; group `name`, `preceding_subtotal`; tag `name`. VDR-TXC-C045 VDR-TXC-C067 VDR-TXC-C079

### D3 Source / technical logic
Price-inclusion: `price_include_override` (stored selection) with company default `account_price_include`; `price_include` is computed, not stored (VDR-TXC-C055 VDR-TXC-C056). Group default: `tax_group_id` is `compute` + `store` + `required` + `precompute` and `readonly=False`; `_compute_tax_group_id` keeps a valid group, otherwise takes the first group (order `sequence asc, id`) for the tax's country and company, else the first country-less group (VDR-TXC-C095 VDR-TXC-C096). Company consistency: `_check_company_auto = True` (VDR-TXC-C094).
State list (none beyond active): `active -> archived [write active=False]`; `archived -> active [write active=True]` (VDR-TXC-C050).
Application constraints (python, not DB): `_constrains_name`, `validate_tax_group_id`, `_constrains_cash_basis_transition_account`, `_validate_repartition_lines`, `_check_children_scope` (VDR-TXC-C097 VDR-TXC-C098 VDR-TXC-C099 VDR-TXC-C100 VDR-TXC-C101). DB-level: source declares 0 `models.Constraint/Index` objects on tax, tax group and distribution line, and the DB has none beyond keys and field indexes (VDR-TXC-C102); the only SQL uniqueness is on tags (VDR-TXC-C366).

### Ten dimensions
| # | Dimension | Finding |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Happy path | Seeded Thai tax set is held in these tables (CAP-TXC-08); runtime creation/edit not exercised. RT. |
| 2 | Reversal / cancel / negative | No stored state; archive via active flag; distribution lines are cascade-deleted with the tax (FK ON DELETE CASCADE) while move lines referencing a tax or distribution line are RESTRICT. VDR-TXC-C050 VDR-TXC-C170 |
| 3 | Multi-company / data scope | Tax, group, distribution line: company column + 1 global company rule each; tags and report definitions shared (no company rule). VDR-TXC-C094 VDR-TXC-C129 |
| 4 | Side effects / cross-module | Link tables to product, order lines, expense, reconcile model lines, accounts, fiscal positions (CAP-TXC-09); uninstalled modules add fields (CAP-TXC-10). |
| 5 | Configuration & optionality | price_include_override, tax_exigibility, cash_basis_transition_account_id, original/replacing taxes are optional. VDR-TXC-C059 VDR-TXC-C060 |
| 6 | Validation & constraints | Python-level only for tax; DB unique on tag (name, applicability, country). VDR-TXC-C102 VDR-TXC-C366 |
| 7 | Roles & permissions | ACL rows: tax 7, group 5, distribution line 4, tag 6 (DB ir_model_access counts; rules above). |
| 8 | Scheduled / automated | NOT APPLICABLE — no cron or automation row is tied to these models in this schema reconciliation (not investigated beyond schema). UNKNOWN for server actions. |
| 9 | Exception & failure | Violations of python constraints raise at write time; not exercised. RT. |
| 10 | Accounting / audit / compliance | Distribution accounts and tags decide where base/tax land and which return grid receives them (see CAP-TXC-02, 04); statutory validation of the Thai set is NOT part of this unit. |

### DB reconciliation
account.tax -> account_tax (BASE TABLE): 63 fields, 27 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 16, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 7, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 4, RELATION TABLE 8, STORED 18, TRANSLATABLE 4; account.tax.group -> account_tax_group (BASE TABLE): 16 fields, 14 columns; ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 1, STORED 7, TRANSLATABLE 2; account.tax.repartition.line -> account_tax_repartition_line (BASE TABLE): 17 fields, 13 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 2, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 1, RELATION TABLE 1, STORED 7; account.account.tag -> account_account_tag (BASE TABLE): 13 fields, 10 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 2, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, STORED 4, TRANSLATABLE 1. SOURCE-ONLY 0, DB-ONLY unexplained 0 for all four (VDR-TXC-C001 VDR-TXC-C002 VDR-TXC-C003 VDR-TXC-C004). Seeded rows: 18 taxes, 5 groups, 72 distribution lines, 16 tags (13 taxes + 3 accounts) = source seed (VDR-TXC-C335 VDR-TXC-C340). Four zero/exempt VAT taxes have empty group in the CSV and carry group `WHT 1%` in DB (VDR-TXC-C337).

### Unknown / Runtime
- Effective group shown on documents/returns for the four zero/exempt VAT taxes. RT.
- Python constraint behaviour (names unique per company/scope/country; repartition set validity). RT.
- Whether statutory grouping of zero-rated/exempt VAT under a withholding group is acceptable: statutory question, out of scope (needs official Thai sources).


## CAP-TXC-02 Tax return and report definition structure

**Function-ID(s):** FUNCTION MAPPING REQUIRED

### D1 Business purpose
Describe returns as data: report -> columns, lines, expressions; the tax-tag engine collects tagged base/tax amounts. VDR-TXC-C117 VDR-TXC-C118 VDR-TXC-C113

### D2 Architecture / data relationships
`account.report` (36 columns of 42 fields) has `country_id`, `root_report_id` (variants), `availability_condition`, `allow_foreign_vat`, `only_tax_exigible`; `column_ids`, `line_ids`, `variant_report_ids` are one2many (VDR-TXC-C103 VDR-TXC-C104 VDR-TXC-C105 VDR-TXC-C106 VDR-TXC-C107). `account.report.line.report_id` (FK->account_report ON DELETE CASCADE), `parent_id`, `code`; `account.report.expression` (`engine`, `formula`, `label`, `subformula`, `date_scope`) hangs on `report_line_id`; `account.report.external.value` stores manual values per company, date and target expression (VDR-TXC-C111 VDR-TXC-C112 VDR-TXC-C122 VDR-TXC-C124 VDR-TXC-C127). Tags link to expressions only through the tag engine (formula text) and the derived `balance_negate`/`report_expression_id` (VDR-TXC-C092 VDR-TXC-C093).

### D3 Source / technical logic
Constraints (DB): line code unique per report, expression label unique per line, `domain` engine requires subformula (VDR-TXC-C389 VDR-TXC-C391 VDR-TXC-C390). Shared reference data: no company column on report, line, expression, column (VDR-TXC-C129).
State: none stored on reports ("active" flag only on `account.report`).

### Ten dimensions
| # | Dimension | Finding |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Happy path | 3 Thai reports seeded (CAP-TXC-08); rendering not exercised. RT. |
| 2 | Reversal / cancel / negative | External values are plain rows; deletion by authorised users; carryover fields exist on expression/external value (carryover_target, carryover_origin_*). VDR-TXC-C128 |
| 3 | Multi-company / data scope | Report definitions shared; external values company-scoped with company rule. VDR-TXC-C129 VDR-TXC-C127 |
| 4 | Side effects / cross-module | Tax tags created from tag-engine formulas; tags attach to distribution lines (CAP-TXC-01) and move lines (CAP-TXC-04). VDR-TXC-C093 |
| 5 | Configuration & optionality | country_id, root_report_id, availability_condition, allow_foreign_vat optional. VDR-TXC-C105 |
| 6 | Validation & constraints | Three DB constraints (above). VDR-TXC-C389 |
| 7 | Roles & permissions | ACL rows: report 3, line 3, expression 3, column 3, external value 2. |
| 8 | Scheduled / automated | NOT APPLICABLE — no scheduled behaviour investigated for the report tables in this unit. |
| 9 | Exception & failure | UNKNOWN — rendering/export failure behaviour not in schema scope. |
| 10 | Accounting / audit / compliance | Return grids derive from tags and formulas; statutory conformance of grids is NOT validated here. |

### DB reconciliation
account.report -> account_report (BASE TABLE): 42 fields, 36 columns; O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 3, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATION TABLE 2, STORED 30, TRANSLATABLE 1; account.report.line -> account_report_line (BASE TABLE): 26 fields, 18 columns; O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 2, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, STORED 17, TRANSLATABLE 1; account.report.expression -> account_report_expression (BASE TABLE): 18 fields, 16 columns; ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, STORED 11, TRANSLATABLE 1; account.report.column -> account_report_column (BASE TABLE): 14 fields, 13 columns; ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, STORED 7, TRANSLATABLE 1; account.report.external.value -> account_report_external_value (BASE TABLE): 17 fields, 13 columns; ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 3, STORED 8. SOURCE-ONLY 0, DB-ONLY unexplained 0 (VDR-TXC-C005 VDR-TXC-C006 VDR-TXC-C007 VDR-TXC-C008 VDR-TXC-C009). Seeded: reports 6 (3 generic + 3 Thai), columns 9, lines 24, expressions 29; engines [['aggregation', 15], ['external', 1], ['tax_tags', 13]]; external values 0 (VDR-TXC-C342).

### Unknown / Runtime
- Return rendering, filters by fiscal position and period. RT.
- How tag signs are applied in practice (derived via SQL join). RT.


## CAP-TXC-03 Fiscal position and mapping data

**Function-ID(s):** FUNCTION MAPPING REQUIRED

### D1 Business purpose
Store rules that substitute taxes and accounts by partner geography/status and make them available on partners, orders, entries. VDR-TXC-C135 VDR-TXC-C133 VDR-TXC-C134

### D2 Architecture / data relationships
`account.fiscal.position` (18 columns of 28 fields): `country_id`, `country_group_id`, `state_ids` (`account_fiscal_position_res_country_state_rel(account_fiscal_position_id,res_country_state_id)`), `zip_from`, `vat_required`, `foreign_vat`, `auto_apply`, `is_domestic`, `company_id`; tax mapping `tax_ids` is the m2m `account_fiscal_position_account_tax_rel(account_fiscal_position_id,account_tax_id)` shared with `account.tax.fiscal_position_ids`; account mapping rows in `account.fiscal.position.account` (`account_src_id`, `account_dest_id`, `position_id` FK->account_fiscal_position ON DELETE CASCADE) (VDR-TXC-C130 VDR-TXC-C131 VDR-TXC-C132 VDR-TXC-C137 VDR-TXC-C138 VDR-TXC-C141 VDR-TXC-C142). References: company `domestic_fiscal_position_id`, `account_purchase_receipt_fiscal_position_id`; partner `property_account_position_id` (company-dependent jsonb); `fiscal_position_id` on sale order, purchase order, journal entry (VDR-TXC-C144 VDR-TXC-C145 VDR-TXC-C146 VDR-TXC-C147 VDR-TXC-C148 VDR-TXC-C149).

### D3 Source / technical logic
DB: unique (position, source, destination) per account mapping (VDR-TXC-C392). Entry/order fiscal-position columns are stored many2one with ON DELETE RESTRICT (`FK->account_fiscal_position ON DELETE RESTRICT`). State: `active` flag only.

### Ten dimensions
| # | Dimension | Finding |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Happy path | Not exercised; no fiscal position exists. RT. |
| 2 | Reversal / cancel / negative | Deleting a position referenced by an entry is RESTRICTed by FK. VDR-TXC-C149 |
| 3 | Multi-company / data scope | Position carries company_id; 1 company rule; mapping rows inherit via position. VDR-TXC-C140 |
| 4 | Side effects / cross-module | Shared link table with tax; states link to country states; used by sales/purchase/entries. VDR-TXC-C137 |
| 5 | Configuration & optionality | All matching criteria optional; auto_apply optional. VDR-TXC-C135 |
| 6 | Validation & constraints | One DB unique on account mapping. VDR-TXC-C392 |
| 7 | Roles & permissions | ACL rows: position 3, mapping 2. |
| 8 | Scheduled / automated | NOT APPLICABLE — none investigated. |
| 9 | Exception & failure | UNKNOWN — runtime selection behaviour. |
| 10 | Accounting / audit / compliance | Substitution changes posted tax/accounts; Thai chart seeds none (VDR-TXC-C150). |

### DB reconciliation
account.fiscal.position -> account_fiscal_position (BASE TABLE): 28 fields, 18 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 4, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 1, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 2, RELATION TABLE 2, STORED 11, TRANSLATABLE 2; account.fiscal.position.account -> account_fiscal_position_account (BASE TABLE): 10 fields, 9 columns; ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 1, STORED 3. SOURCE-ONLY 0, DB-ONLY unexplained 0 (VDR-TXC-C010 VDR-TXC-C011). Seeded rows: 0 positions, 0 mappings; `account_fiscal_position_account_tax_rel` 0 rows (VDR-TXC-C150).

### Unknown / Runtime
- Automatic selection logic and effects. RT.
- Whether Thai operation needs positions for foreign/export/exempt customers: business question, not decided from source absence (NATIVE status UNKNOWN until FDS).


## CAP-TXC-04 Journal entry, entry line, reconciliation and journal tax and currency data

**Function-ID(s):** FUNCTION MAPPING REQUIRED (lock-date rows: PCO-F01)

### D1 Business purpose
Store posted accounting data and the tax/currency attributes that returns, reversal, reconciliation and cash-basis need. VDR-TXC-C159 VDR-TXC-C169 VDR-TXC-C173

### D2 Architecture / data relationships
- `account.move` (80 columns of 227 fields): dates `date`, `invoice_date`, `invoice_date_due`, `taxable_supply_date`, `delivery_date`; currency `currency_id`, stored `invoice_currency_rate`; stored `amount_untaxed`, `amount_tax`, `amount_total`; derived (non-stored) `tax_totals`, `tax_calculation_rounding_method` (related to company); `tax_country_id`; `fiscal_position_id`; cash-basis `tax_cash_basis_origin_move_id`, `tax_cash_basis_rec_id`, `always_tax_exigible`; `invoice_cash_rounding_id`; `reversed_entry_id` (VDR-TXC-C151 VDR-TXC-C152 VDR-TXC-C154 VDR-TXC-C155 VDR-TXC-C157 VDR-TXC-C158 VDR-TXC-C160 VDR-TXC-C161 VDR-TXC-C163 VDR-TXC-C162 VDR-TXC-C165 VDR-TXC-C166 VDR-TXC-C164 VDR-TXC-C168).
- `account.move.line` (63 columns of 113 fields): `debit`, `credit`, `balance`, `amount_currency`, `currency_id`, `currency_rate`, `tax_base_amount`, `tax_repartition_line_id` (FK->account_tax_repartition_line ON DELETE RESTRICT), `tax_line_id` (related to repartition line, stored, FK->account_tax ON DELETE RESTRICT), `tax_group_id` (related, stored), `group_tax_id`, `deductible_amount`, `extra_tax_data` (jsonb); `tax_ids` -> `account_move_line_account_tax_rel(account_move_line_id,account_tax_id)`; `tax_tag_ids` -> `account_account_tag_account_move_line_rel(account_move_line_id,account_account_tag_id)` (VDR-TXC-C174 VDR-TXC-C172 VDR-TXC-C170 VDR-TXC-C171 VDR-TXC-C175 VDR-TXC-C176 VDR-TXC-C180 VDR-TXC-C177 VDR-TXC-C179 VDR-TXC-C169 VDR-TXC-C173).
- Reconciliation: partial (`debit_move_id`, `credit_move_id` RESTRICT, `amount`, per-side currency amounts, `exchange_move_id`, `full_reconcile_id`, `draft_caba_move_vals`) and full (o2m only; no column of its own besides ORM stamps) (VDR-TXC-C185 VDR-TXC-C187 VDR-TXC-C190 VDR-TXC-C192 VDR-TXC-C193).
- Lock exceptions `account.lock_exception` (13 columns): `lock_date_field`, `lock_date`, `user_id`, `end_datetime`, `reason`, `company_id`; per-lock-type dates are derived non-stored (VDR-TXC-C195 VDR-TXC-C196 VDR-TXC-C197 VDR-TXC-C198 VDR-TXC-C199 VDR-TXC-C200).
- Cash-basis settings on company and rounding: `tax_cash_basis_journal_id`, `account_cash_basis_base_account_id`; `account.cash.rounding` (`rounding`, `strategy`; loss/profit accounts company-dependent) (VDR-TXC-C202 VDR-TXC-C203 VDR-TXC-C204 VDR-TXC-C205 VDR-TXC-C206).

### D3 Source / technical logic
DB constraints on lines: VDR-TXC-C377 VDR-TXC-C378 VDR-TXC-C379 VDR-TXC-C380; on moves: unique posted name per journal (VDR-TXC-C369); journal code unique per company (VDR-TXC-C375). Sequence and analytic indexes are created by `init()` code (VDR-TXC-C401 VDR-TXC-C402). State list (schema level): `draft -> posted -> cancel` is held in the stored `state` column; transition logic belongs to U11/U12 and is not re-derived here.

### Ten dimensions
| # | Dimension | Finding |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Happy path | No entries exist; columns reviewed structurally. RT. VDR-TXC-C207 |
| 2 | Reversal / cancel / negative | `reversed_entry_id` stored; sign check on balance vs foreign amount; RESTRICT FKs from lines to tax/distribution line. VDR-TXC-C168 VDR-TXC-C378 |
| 3 | Multi-company / data scope | Company column + 9 rules (move) / 9 (line); reconcile models and payments company-scoped. VDR-TXC-C012 VDR-TXC-C013 |
| 4 | Side effects / cross-module | Stored totals and tax lines feed returns; analytic gin index; sequence index. VDR-TXC-C401 VDR-TXC-C402 |
| 5 | Configuration & optionality | Taxable-supply date, delivery date, cash rounding, cash-basis are optional. VDR-TXC-C154 VDR-TXC-C167 |
| 6 | Validation & constraints | Four line checks; unique posted name; journal code unique. VDR-TXC-C377 VDR-TXC-C369 |
| 7 | Roles & permissions | ACL rows: move 7, line 8, partial 6, full 3, journal 9, lock exception 2. |
| 8 | Scheduled / automated | NOT APPLICABLE — scheduled auto-posting is U11/B01 territory; not re-derived. UNKNOWN for this unit. |
| 9 | Exception & failure | Constraint violations raise DB errors on write; not exercised. RT. |
| 10 | Accounting / audit / compliance | Tax/base/tag columns on lines are the sole stored basis for returns; deductible amount and journal non-deductible account support non-deductible treatment. VDR-TXC-C183 VDR-TXC-C201 |

### DB reconciliation
account.move -> account_move (BASE TABLE): 227 fields, 80 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 101, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 20, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 12, RELATION TABLE 5, STORED 83; account.move.line -> account_move_line (BASE TABLE): 113 fields, 63 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 27, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 4, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 24, RELATION TABLE 3, STORED 48, TRANSLATABLE 1; account.partial.reconcile -> account_partial_reconcile (BASE TABLE): 19 fields, 17 columns; ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 3, STORED 10; account.full.reconcile -> account_full_reconcile (BASE TABLE): 8 fields, 5 columns; O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 2, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6; account.journal -> account_journal (BASE TABLE): 100 fields, 35 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 41, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 8, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 12, RELATION TABLE 2, STORED 30, TRANSLATABLE 1; account.lock_exception -> account_lock_exception (BASE TABLE): 19 fields, 13 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 5, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, STORED 8; account.cash.rounding -> account_cash_rounding (BASE TABLE): 12 fields, 11 columns; COMPANY-DEPENDENT 2, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, STORED 3, TRANSLATABLE 1; account.reconcile.model -> account_reconcile_model (BASE TABLE): 35 fields, 18 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 9, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 5, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATION TABLE 2, STORED 12, TRANSLATABLE 1; account.reconcile.model.line -> account_reconcile_model_line (BASE TABLE): 19 fields, 15 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 1, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 1, RELATION TABLE 1, STORED 9, TRANSLATABLE 1. All SOURCE-ONLY 0 / DB-ONLY unexplained 0 (VDR-TXC-C012 VDR-TXC-C013 VDR-TXC-C014 VDR-TXC-C015 VDR-TXC-C018 VDR-TXC-C020 VDR-TXC-C021 VDR-TXC-C022 VDR-TXC-C023). Rows: moves 0, lines 0, partial 0, full 0, lock exceptions 0, reconcile models 2 (lines 2), journals 7 (VDR-TXC-C207).

## CAP-TXC-05 Accounts and payments including withholding-related columns

**Function-ID(s):** FUNCTION MAPPING REQUIRED

### D1 Business purpose
Keep the account flags/defaults that drive tax behaviour, payment attributes, and (where present) withholding data. VDR-TXC-C208 VDR-TXC-C211 VDR-TXC-C218

### D2 Architecture / data relationships
`account.account` (16 columns of 60 fields): stored `account_type`, `reconcile`, `non_trade`, `currency_id`; `code_store` company-dependent jsonb (displayed `code` non-stored); `tax_ids` (`account_account_tax_default_rel(account_id,tax_id)`), `tag_ids` (`account_account_account_tag(account_account_id,account_account_tag_id)`), `company_ids` (`account_account_res_company_rel(account_account_id,res_company_id)`) (VDR-TXC-C209 VDR-TXC-C210 VDR-TXC-C213 VDR-TXC-C212 VDR-TXC-C214 VDR-TXC-C215). `account.payment` (34 columns of 90): `payment_type`, `partner_type`, `amount`, `currency_id`, `date`, `journal_id`, `payment_method_line_id` (redeclared by check printing), `outstanding_account_id`, `destination_account_id`, `move_id` (FK->account_move ON DELETE SET NULL) (VDR-TXC-C216 VDR-TXC-C217 VDR-TXC-C219 VDR-TXC-C220 VDR-TXC-C221 VDR-TXC-C222 VDR-TXC-C223 VDR-TXC-C224 VDR-TXC-C225).

### D3 Source / technical logic
**Withholding-related columns:** a scan of every public table and every field row for `withhold`, `wht` and `pnd` returned nothing; the payment table has no withholding column (VDR-TXC-C226). The Thai withholding set is carried as negative-rate taxes (CAP-TXC-08); the optional payment-time withholding module is not installed (CAP-TXC-10). Non-deductible treatment: journal `non_deductible_account_id`, line `deductible_amount` (VDR-TXC-C201 VDR-TXC-C183). DB: payment amount check >= 0 (VDR-TXC-C386).
Classification for the withholding requirement: `NATIVE GAP / EXTENSION REQUIRED` candidate for payment-level withholding amounts, certificates and form categories (PARTIAL only through ordinary taxes); statutory content NOT validated here.

### Ten dimensions
| # | Dimension | Finding |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Happy path | Not exercised (no payments). RT. |
| 2 | Reversal / cancel / negative | Payment amount cannot be negative at DB level; direction held in payment_type. VDR-TXC-C386 |
| 3 | Multi-company / data scope | Account is shared across companies via company_ids (no company column); payment has company_id and 1 rule. VDR-TXC-C214 |
| 4 | Side effects / cross-module | Payment links to a journal entry; check printing redeclares method line. VDR-TXC-C225 VDR-TXC-C222 |
| 5 | Configuration & optionality | Withholding at payment is optional and not installed (CAP-TXC-10). VDR-TXC-C226 |
| 6 | Validation & constraints | DB: payment amount check; accounts have no DB unique in declarations. VDR-TXC-C386 |
| 7 | Roles & permissions | ACL rows: account 8, payment 2. |
| 8 | Scheduled / automated | NOT APPLICABLE. |
| 9 | Exception & failure | UNKNOWN — runtime. |
| 10 | Accounting / audit / compliance | Absence of stored withholding data is a gap indicator, not proof that Thai requirements are met or unmet (Community absence is not proof of no requirement). |

### DB reconciliation
account.account -> account_account (BASE TABLE): 60 fields, 16 columns; COMPANY-DEPENDENT 1, NON-STORED COMPUTED 30, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 6, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 3, RELATION TABLE 3, STORED 9, TRANSLATABLE 2; account.payment -> account_payment (BASE TABLE): 90 fields, 34 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 37, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 6, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 12, RELATION TABLE 1, STORED 28; account.code.mapping -> account_code_mapping (no table): 5 fields, 0 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 3, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 2 (query-defined, no table). SOURCE-ONLY 0, DB-ONLY unexplained 0 (VDR-TXC-C016 VDR-TXC-C019 VDR-TXC-C017). Seeded accounts 147 (144 from CSV + 3 created for journals) (VDR-TXC-C341).

### Unknown / Runtime
- Whether Thai withholding certificates/forms need stored structure: statutory question; extension decision belongs to FDS.


## CAP-TXC-06 Partner, company, product, country and currency tax configuration, with lock dates

**Function-ID(s):** FUNCTION MAPPING REQUIRED; lock-date rows PCO-F01; fiscal-year rows PCO-F02

### D1 Business purpose
Hold the master-data and company settings that drive tax: tax numbers and registration (partner), fiscal country, rounding, defaults and lock dates (company), taxes and accounts (product/category), currency rounding and rates. VDR-TXC-C227 VDR-TXC-C235 VDR-TXC-C272 VDR-TXC-C250

### D2 Architecture / data relationships
- `res.partner` (105 columns of 247 fields): `vat` and `company_registry` plain varchar (redeclared by mail for tracking), `country_id`, `is_company`; `l10n_th_branch_name` non-stored computed; properties stored as company-dependent jsonb: `property_account_position_id`, `property_account_receivable_id`, `property_account_payable_id`, `property_payment_term_id` (VDR-TXC-C227 VDR-TXC-C228 VDR-TXC-C229 VDR-TXC-C230 VDR-TXC-C234 VDR-TXC-C146 VDR-TXC-C231 VDR-TXC-C232 VDR-TXC-C233). No stored branch field exists (CAP-TXC-08).
- `res.company` (178 columns of 239 fields): `account_fiscal_country_id`, `tax_calculation_rounding_method`, `account_price_include`, `tax_exigibility`, `account_sale_tax_id`, `account_purchase_tax_id`, `fiscalyear_last_day/month`, exchange journal/accounts, `chart_template`, `anglo_saxon_accounting`, `vat`, `company_registry` (related to partner) (VDR-TXC-C235 VDR-TXC-C236 VDR-TXC-C237 VDR-TXC-C238 VDR-TXC-C239 VDR-TXC-C240 VDR-TXC-C241 VDR-TXC-C242 VDR-TXC-C243 VDR-TXC-C244 VDR-TXC-C245 VDR-TXC-C246 VDR-TXC-C247 VDR-TXC-C248 VDR-TXC-C249).
- Lock dates, five stored date columns: `sale_lock_date`, `purchase_lock_date`, `tax_lock_date`, `fiscalyear_lock_date`, `hard_lock_date`; per-user variants `user_*_lock_date` are non-stored computed (VDR-TXC-C270 VDR-TXC-C271 VDR-TXC-C272 VDR-TXC-C273 VDR-TXC-C274 VDR-TXC-C275 VDR-TXC-C276 VDR-TXC-C277).
- Product: `product.template.taxes_id` -> `product_taxes_rel(prod_id,tax_id)`, `supplier_taxes_id` -> `product_supplier_taxes_rel(prod_id,tax_id)`, `account_tag_ids` -> `account_account_tag_product_template_rel(product_template_id,account_account_tag_id)`, income/expense accounts company-dependent; `product.product` reaches taxes through `_inherits` delegation (no own column: product.product -> product_product (BASE TABLE): 220 fields, 18 columns; COMPANY-DEPENDENT 1, NON-STORED COMPUTED 80, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 17, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 5, RELATED 93, RELATION TABLE 3, STORED 13, TRANSLATABLE 8); category: `property_account_income_categ_id`, `property_account_expense_categ_id` (company-dependent) (VDR-TXC-C250 VDR-TXC-C251 VDR-TXC-C254 VDR-TXC-C252 VDR-TXC-C253 VDR-TXC-C255 VDR-TXC-C258 VDR-TXC-C259 VDR-TXC-C256 VDR-TXC-C257).
- Currency: `res.currency.rounding`, `decimal_places`; `res.currency.rate` (`rate`, `currency_id`, `company_id`); country `code`, `vat_label`; group/state (VDR-TXC-C260 VDR-TXC-C261 VDR-TXC-C262 VDR-TXC-C263 VDR-TXC-C264 VDR-TXC-C265 VDR-TXC-C266 VDR-TXC-C267 VDR-TXC-C268).

### D3 Source / technical logic
DB: unique currency name, rounding > 0, rate > 0, unique rate per name/currency/company, unique country name and code, unique state per country+code (VDR-TXC-C352 VDR-TXC-C353 VDR-TXC-C355 VDR-TXC-C354 VDR-TXC-C348 VDR-TXC-C349 VDR-TXC-C351). Company-dependent fields are jsonb columns keyed by company (Odoo 19 has no property table; DB `res_partner` company-dependent columns: 22, `product_template`: 10, `product_category`: 8). State: none (company active flag not examined). Lock dates in this DB: all five NULL (VDR-TXC-C272). Tax-number validation modules (`base_vat`) are uninstalled, so `vat` carries no validation flag (VDR-TXC-C490).

### Ten dimensions
| # | Dimension | Finding |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Happy path | Not exercised. RT. |
| 2 | Reversal / cancel / negative | Lock exceptions allow temporary override (CAP-TXC-04); lock dates are plain dates. VDR-TXC-C274 |
| 3 | Multi-company / data scope | Per-company jsonb properties on partner/product/category; company rules: partner 2, company 4, product template 2. Single company in this DB. VDR-TXC-C146 |
| 4 | Side effects / cross-module | company defaults seed new taxes/products; stock valuation flags share the company table. VDR-TXC-C247 |
| 5 | Configuration & optionality | rounding method, price-inclusion default, cash basis, defaults, lock dates, fiscal year end. VDR-TXC-C236 VDR-TXC-C237 |
| 6 | Validation & constraints | Currency/country DB constraints (above); partner has check on contact name only. VDR-TXC-C356 |
| 7 | Roles & permissions | ACL rows: partner 16, company 4, product template 6, currency 5. |
| 8 | Scheduled / automated | NOT APPLICABLE for schema; currency rate cron not investigated. |
| 9 | Exception & failure | UNKNOWN. |
| 10 | Accounting / audit / compliance | Lock dates are the stored cut-off controls (PCO-F01); fiscal year end day/month stored (PCO-F02); Thai branch is derived, not stored. |

## CAP-TXC-07 Order, purchase, expense and stock line tax and currency columns

**Function-ID(s):** FUNCTION MAPPING REQUIRED

### D1 Business purpose
Document the stored tax/currency columns that sales, purchase, expense and stock records hand to accounting. VDR-TXC-C285 VDR-TXC-C302 VDR-TXC-C311 VDR-TXC-C321

### D2 Architecture / data relationships
- `sale.order` (60 columns of 163): `fiscal_position_id`, `currency_id`, `currency_rate`, stored `amount_untaxed/amount_tax/amount_total`; `tax_totals` and `tax_calculation_rounding_method` not stored (VDR-TXC-C278 VDR-TXC-C279 VDR-TXC-C280 VDR-TXC-C281 VDR-TXC-C282 VDR-TXC-C284 VDR-TXC-C283).
- `sale.order.line` (63 columns of 125): `tax_ids` -> `account_tax_sale_order_line_rel(sale_order_line_id,account_tax_id)`, `price_unit`, `discount`, stored `price_subtotal/price_tax/price_total`, `currency_id` (related, stored), `qty_invoiced`, `extra_tax_data`, `tax_country_id` (VDR-TXC-C286 VDR-TXC-C287 VDR-TXC-C288 VDR-TXC-C289 VDR-TXC-C290 VDR-TXC-C291 VDR-TXC-C292 VDR-TXC-C293 VDR-TXC-C294).
- `purchase.order` / `purchase.order.line`: same pattern plus company-currency totals `amount_total_cc`, `price_total_cc`; line `tax_ids` -> `account_tax_purchase_order_line_rel(purchase_order_line_id,account_tax_id)` (VDR-TXC-C295 VDR-TXC-C296 VDR-TXC-C297 VDR-TXC-C298 VDR-TXC-C299 VDR-TXC-C300 VDR-TXC-C301 VDR-TXC-C303 VDR-TXC-C304 VDR-TXC-C305 VDR-TXC-C306 VDR-TXC-C307 VDR-TXC-C308 VDR-TXC-C309 VDR-TXC-C310).
- `hr.expense`: `tax_ids` -> `expense_tax(expense_id,tax_id)`, `tax_amount`, `tax_amount_currency`, `untaxed_amount(_currency)`, `total_amount(_currency)`, `currency_id`, `account_move_id` (VDR-TXC-C312 VDR-TXC-C313 VDR-TXC-C314 VDR-TXC-C315 VDR-TXC-C316 VDR-TXC-C317 VDR-TXC-C318 VDR-TXC-C319 VDR-TXC-C320).
- `stock.move`: `price_unit` (declared by stock, redeclared by stock valuation), `value`, `company_currency_id` related; no tax column (VDR-TXC-C321 VDR-TXC-C322 VDR-TXC-C323 VDR-TXC-C324).

### D3 Source / technical logic
DB: sales/purchase line "accountable"/"non-accountable" checks, confirmed order requires date (VDR-TXC-C395 VDR-TXC-C396 VDR-TXC-C399 VDR-TXC-C400 VDR-TXC-C393). Stored totals are computed + stored; recomputation is runtime. State: held in the order `state` column; not re-derived.

### Ten dimensions
| # | Dimension | Finding |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Happy path | No orders, lines, expenses or stock moves exist. RT. |
| 2 | Reversal / cancel / negative | Not applicable at schema level; reversal data live on entries (CAP-TXC-04). |
| 3 | Multi-company / data scope | company_id + company rules: sale order 4, order line 6, purchase 2, expense 5, stock move 2. |
| 4 | Side effects / cross-module | Order line taxes flow to invoice lines; expense generates entries; stock valuation reads price_unit/value. VDR-TXC-C320 VDR-TXC-C542 |
| 5 | Configuration & optionality | price_include and rounding method read from company; tax links optional per line. VDR-TXC-C283 |
| 6 | Validation & constraints | Line shape checks, confirmed-order date check. VDR-TXC-C395 VDR-TXC-C399 |
| 7 | Roles & permissions | ACL rows: sale order 10, line 9, purchase 6, line 6, expense 4, stock move 10. |
| 8 | Scheduled / automated | NOT APPLICABLE for schema. |
| 9 | Exception & failure | UNKNOWN — runtime. |
| 10 | Accounting / audit / compliance | Stored subtotals/tax amounts are rounding-dependent; Thai VAT/withholding on these lines is the tax-link set (CAP-TXC-08). |

### DB reconciliation
sale.order -> sale_order (BASE TABLE): 163 fields, 60 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 70, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 11, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 5, RELATED 9, RELATION TABLE 6, STORED 62; sale.order.line -> sale_order_line (BASE TABLE): 125 fields, 63 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 30, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 11, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 18, RELATION TABLE 5, STORED 55; purchase.order -> purchase_order (BASE TABLE): 104 fields, 43 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 33, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 6, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 14, RELATION TABLE 3, STORED 42; purchase.order.line -> purchase_order_line (BASE TABLE): 67 fields, 35 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 12, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 2, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 15, RELATION TABLE 3, STORED 29; hr.expense -> hr_expense (BASE TABLE): 84 fields, 37 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 31, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 6, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 7, RELATION TABLE 1, STORED 33; stock.move -> stock_move (BASE TABLE): 123 fields, 69 columns; NON-STORED COMPUTED 27, O2M INVERSE (non-stored) 4, ORM-implicit DB-ONLY 6, RELATED 14, RELATION TABLE 7, STORED 65. SOURCE-ONLY 0, DB-ONLY unexplained 0 (VDR-TXC-C033 VDR-TXC-C034 VDR-TXC-C035 VDR-TXC-C036 VDR-TXC-C043 VDR-TXC-C037). Rows: all transactional tables 0 (VDR-TXC-C207).

## CAP-TXC-08 Thai localisation additions and seeded Thai tax configuration

**Function-ID(s):** FUNCTION MAPPING REQUIRED

### D1 Business purpose
Make a Thailand-registered company usable out of the box: chart, 18 taxes, tax-return reports and a QR-bank proxy list; identify exactly what `l10n_th` adds to the schema. VDR-TXC-C325 VDR-TXC-C327 VDR-TXC-C334

### D2 Architecture / data relationships
- `l10n_th` declares **two** fields in source (B01 counted `src_fields` 2, `db_fields` 14): `res.partner.l10n_th_branch_name` (Char, compute, not stored; also visible on `res.users` via delegation) and `res.partner.bank.proxy_type` (`selection_add` of three values, `ondelete` set default). DB rows owned by `l10n_th`: 14 `ir.model.fields` (ids/display_name stubs for 5 models, 2 `l10n_th_branch_name`, 2 `proxy_type`), 3 selection values (VDR-TXC-C325 VDR-TXC-C327 VDR-TXC-C328 VDR-TXC-C329). No stored column, table or SQL constraint is added.
- Seed data: 4 CSV (accounts 144 rows, taxes 72 lines = 18 taxes x 4 distribution lines, tax groups 5, assets 12) + tax-report XML (355 lines) + company defaults in `template_th.py` (VDR-TXC-C341 VDR-TXC-C335 VDR-TXC-C340 VDR-TXC-C342 VDR-TXC-C343).
- Withholding is modelled only as taxes: 8 purchase (company PND53, individual PND3; 1/2/3/5%), 4 sale (tax-excluded; 114300), group per rate; zero/exempt VAT taxes carry no CSV group (VDR-TXC-C338 VDR-TXC-C339 VDR-TXC-C344 VDR-TXC-C337).

### D3 Source / technical logic
Template loader: company-level template data sets `account_fiscal_country_id` (Thailand), default sale tax `tax_output_vat`, purchase tax `tax_input_vat`, `tax_exigibility` (string 'True'), exchange/suspense/discount accounts (VDR-TXC-C330 VDR-TXC-C331 VDR-TXC-C332 VDR-TXC-C333). Branch: label `Branch <registry>` / `Headquarter` only for company partners with country TH (VDR-TXC-C326). Proxy validation: `_check_th_proxy` (python) (VDR-TXC-C328).
Seed vs DB comparison (executed by script): taxes 18/18, distribution lines 72/72, groups 5/5, accounts 144/147 (3 extra created for journals), attribute comparisons 486 with differences: 4 (group of the four zero/exempt VAT taxes) (VDR-TXC-C335 VDR-TXC-C337).

### Ten dimensions
| # | Dimension | Finding |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Happy path | Install on a TH company loads chart/taxes/reports (U24). DB reflects it. VDR-TXC-C335 |
| 2 | Reversal / cancel / negative | Reload/replace behaviour is U24; not schema-level. |
| 3 | Multi-company / data scope | Seed rows are loaded per company with company-prefixed ids; single company here. VDR-TXC-C334 |
| 4 | Side effects / cross-module | Company defaults point taxes; report XML creates tags; asset CSV has no target model. VDR-TXC-C330 VDR-TXC-C343 |
| 5 | Configuration & optionality | Withholding taxes are optional data rows; fiscal positions absent. VDR-TXC-C150 |
| 6 | Validation & constraints | Only application-level proxy validation; no DB constraint added. VDR-TXC-C328 |
| 7 | Roles & permissions | No ACL or rule is declared by l10n_th (manifest data: report XML and view only). VDR-TXC-C150 |
| 8 | Scheduled / automated | NOT APPLICABLE — no cron in l10n_th. |
| 9 | Exception & failure | UNKNOWN — runtime. |
| 10 | Accounting / audit / compliance | Statutory adequacy of the seeded VAT/withholding set and of the branch handling is NOT assessed here (needs official Thai sources); native status of withholding: PARTIAL (taxes only) / `NATIVE GAP / EXTENSION REQUIRED` for payment-level handling. |

### DB reconciliation
| Seed item | Source seed | DB rows | Result |
|---|---|---|---|
| account.tax | 18 | 18 | 486 attribute comparisons (taxes and distribution lines); 4 group defaults differ (see above) |
| account.tax.repartition.line | 72 | 72 | type, document, account, tags, closing flag identical |
| account.tax.group | 5 | 5 | name, country, payable/receivable accounts identical |
| account.account | 144 | 147 | 144 identical (type, reconcile, non-trade); 3 DB-only created by journal logic |
| account.report (l10n_th) | 3 | 3 | identical counts |
| account.report.line (l10n_th) | 24 | 24 | identical counts |
| account.report.expression (l10n_th) | 24 | 24 (+5 from account module) | 13 tag-engine, 10 aggregation, 1 external overall in l10n_th set |
| account.report.column (l10n_th) | 3 | 3 | identical counts |
| account.account.tag | 13 tag-engine expressions | 13 taxes-tags + 3 account-tags | tags unsigned; sign via formula text |
| account.fiscal.position | 0 | 0 | consistent |
| account.asset (CSV) | 12 | no table | SOURCE-ONLY: model not installed |
| res.company defaults | template_th.py | 1 row | consistent: fiscal country, default taxes, rounding |

### Unknown / Runtime
- Statutory adequacy of the seeded set. Outside this unit (official Thai sources required).
- Effect of the asset CSV when an asset model is installed later. UNKNOWN.


## CAP-TXC-09 Constraint, index, key and relation-table reconciliation

**Function-ID(s):** FUNCTION MAPPING REQUIRED

### D1 Business purpose
Prove which declared database rules actually exist and explain every other database object on the tax-path tables. VDR-TXC-C405 VDR-TXC-C404

### D2 Architecture / data relationships
Source declares `models.Constraint` (CHECK/UNIQUE), `models.Index`, `models.UniqueIndex` as class attributes named `_x`; DB object name = `<table>_x` (`make_identifier`, 63 chars + crc32 suffix when long). No legacy `_sql_constraints` remains (0 found). Across all installed modules (table-backed models): 272 Constraint, 29 UniqueIndex, 58 Index declared; DB holds 272, 29, 58 (none absent). `res.device` (`_auto = False` view) inherits 2 index declarations from `res.device.log`, applied on `res_device_log`; 3 DB CHECK constraints on non-tax tables have no `models.Constraint` attribute found by the scan (VDR-TXC-C405 VDR-TXC-C406). For the 44 reviewed models: 56 declared objects, 56 present (register below). Extra DB objects on these tables: 260 indexes (207 field-level, 41 primary keys, 12 other = sequence x2, analytic gin x5, phone x4 and 1 hashed-name property index), all explained (VDR-TXC-C404 VDR-TXC-C401 VDR-TXC-C402 VDR-TXC-C403).
Foreign keys follow field definitions (`ondelete`: default SET NULL, RESTRICT when required); company-dependent many2one (jsonb) and references to `ir.actions.actions` have no FK (VDR-TXC-C407 VDR-TXC-C408 VDR-TXC-C146 VDR-TXC-C409). ORM-required fields with a column: 150; NOT NULL in DB: 150 (VDR-TXC-C410). Link tables: 67 stored many2many tables reconciled; 66 have PK + 2 FKs + 2 NOT NULL + reverse index (the remaining one is backed by a full record table); of those 58 cascade on both sides and 8 have a RESTRICT side by field `ondelete` (VDR-TXC-C416 VDR-TXC-C419 VDR-TXC-C420; see register).
Virtual models: VDR-TXC-C411 VDR-TXC-C412 VDR-TXC-C413.

### D3 Source / technical logic
Constraints applied at module update by `Constraint.apply_to_database` / `Index.apply_to_database` (`odoo/orm/table_objects.py`); index definitions are stored as a comment on the index. `init()` hooks create indexes outside declarations (sequence mixin, analytic mixin, phone mixin) (VDR-TXC-C401 VDR-TXC-C402 VDR-TXC-C403). State: not applicable.

### Ten dimensions
| # | Dimension | Finding |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Happy path | All declared objects present for reviewed models. VDR-TXC-C405 |
| 2 | Reversal / cancel / negative | RESTRICT FKs prevent deleting referenced taxes/distribution lines/positions (see register). VDR-TXC-C170 |
| 3 | Multi-company / data scope | No SQL object is company-specific except unique indexes that include company_id (currency rate, journal code, tag country). VDR-TXC-C354 VDR-TXC-C375 |
| 4 | Side effects / cross-module | Indexes from analytic/phone/sequence mixins appear on tax-path tables. VDR-TXC-C402 VDR-TXC-C401 |
| 5 | Configuration & optionality | Trigram-based indexes depend on DB extension availability. VDR-TXC-C403 |
| 6 | Validation & constraints | See register: 56 declared objects, all present. VDR-TXC-C405 |
| 7 | Roles & permissions | NOT APPLICABLE. |
| 8 | Scheduled / automated | NOT APPLICABLE; `init()` runs at module update only. |
| 9 | Exception & failure | Violations raise PostgreSQL errors translated by the ORM; not exercised. RT. |
| 10 | Accounting / audit / compliance | Journal-entry integrity checks (credit*debit=0, sign, accountable fields) are DB-enforced. VDR-TXC-C377 |

### DB reconciliation
See registers `Constraint and Index Reconciliation` and `Relation Table Reconciliation` below. Result: declared-in-source vs DB: 0 absent for reviewed models; DB-only objects on reviewed tables explained (0 unexplained); DB tables not tied to a model or relation: 7 (`orm_signaling_*`, platform infrastructure, not tax); relation table referenced but absent: 1 (`im_livechat_channel_member_history_conversation_tag_rel`, not tax); model tables absent for non-abstract models: query-defined or abstract only.

### Unknown / Runtime
- Constraint failure messages and handling. RT.

## B01 verification and extension

Earlier baseline: B01 (`03_DB_RECONCILIATION/B01_SOURCE_DB_RECONCILIATION_AND_L0_CONFIG_CONTEXT.md`, per-module TSV). Verified for the 44 tax-path models: **confirmed** — no source-declared field/model is missing in the DB (0), no unexplained DB-only field (0); B01's seeded counts (147 accounts, 18 taxes, 7 journals, 5 tax groups, 2 reconcile models; l10n_th 2 source fields vs 14 DB fields) reproduce exactly; company observations (tax rounding global, prices tax-excluded, lock-date fields on the company, periodic valuation with anglo-saxon flag off) reproduce (VDR-TXC-C269). **Extended** — this unit adds: type/store/company-dependent/translatable/required/index agreement per field (44 models, 3183 fields: 0 mismatches), constraint/index presence (56 of 56; global 272/272 Constraint, 29/29 UniqueIndex, 58/58 Index for table-backed models), foreign-key and NOT NULL behaviour, 67 link tables, the uninstalled tax-path source, and the seeded Thai tax attribute comparison (486 attribute comparisons, CAP-TXC-08). **Contradictions with B01:** none found. **Observation to carry forward:** B01 reported Thai field count `src_fields 2`; this scan also finds 2. B01's total of 12,223 statically declared fields is below the 12,335 raw field declarations this scan finds in the 356 installed modules (26 modules differ by 112 fields in total). Two causes were traced: 18 annotated-assignment field declarations in `base` (for example `base/models/res_partner.py:150`), and models under `report/` in `hr_skills` (20 of its 21 extra fields); the cause of the remaining difference was not traced (UNKNOWN). None of the traced differences touches a database mismatch: the DB-side registry is the authority used here. VDR-TXC-C012 VDR-TXC-C027 VDR-TXC-C543 VDR-TXC-C325 VDR-TXC-C327

## REGISTER: Business Rules

| BR-ID | Rule (neutral) | Condition / configuration | Claim-IDs | Class |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| BR-TXC-01 | A tax must belong to a tax group; an empty group is filled with the first group of matching country and company, else the first generic group | always (compute stored required) | VDR-TXC-C095 VDR-TXC-C096 | FACT |
| BR-TXC-02 | Zero-rate and exempt Thai VAT taxes have no group in seed data and take the first group found | th chart seed + BR-TXC-01 | VDR-TXC-C337 | OBSERVATION |
| BR-TXC-03 | Price-inclusion is an optional per-tax override, defaulting to the company choice; the effective flag is not stored | always | VDR-TXC-C055 VDR-TXC-C056 VDR-TXC-C237 | FACT |
| BR-TXC-04 | A tag is unique by name, applicability and country | always (DB unique) | VDR-TXC-C366 | FACT |
| BR-TXC-05 | A report line code is unique within a report; an expression label is unique within a line; a domain-engine expression needs a sub-formula | always (DB) | VDR-TXC-C389 VDR-TXC-C391 VDR-TXC-C390 | FACT |
| BR-TXC-06 | An entry line cannot carry both a debit and a credit; balance and foreign amount share a sign; accountable lines require an account; heading and note lines carry no amount or account | always (DB) | VDR-TXC-C377 VDR-TXC-C378 VDR-TXC-C379 VDR-TXC-C380 | FACT |
| BR-TXC-07 | A posted entry name is unique within its journal; a journal code is unique within a company | always (DB) | VDR-TXC-C369 VDR-TXC-C375 | FACT |
| BR-TXC-08 | Five period lock dates are stored on the company; exceptions are a separate store; per-user values are derived | always | VDR-TXC-C270 VDR-TXC-C272 VDR-TXC-C274 VDR-TXC-C196 VDR-TXC-C276 | FACT |
| BR-TXC-09 | The Thai branch label is derived from the registration number and is not stored | l10n_th installed | VDR-TXC-C325 VDR-TXC-C326 | FACT |
| BR-TXC-10 | Thai withholding is represented only as negative-rate taxes with tags and accounts; no stored payment-level withholding structure exists in the installed set | l10n_th installed; withholding module uninstalled | VDR-TXC-C338 VDR-TXC-C339 VDR-TXC-C226 | INFERENCE |
| BR-TXC-11 | A payment amount cannot be negative | always (DB) | VDR-TXC-C386 | FACT |
| BR-TXC-12 | A currency rate must be positive and unique per name, currency and company; a currency rounding must be positive | always (DB) | VDR-TXC-C355 VDR-TXC-C354 VDR-TXC-C353 | FACT |
| BR-TXC-13 | Partner fiscal position, receivable and payable accounts are per-company properties held as per-company maps in one column | always | VDR-TXC-C146 VDR-TXC-C231 | FACT |
| BR-TXC-14 | Stock movements carry no tax column | always | VDR-TXC-C324 | INFERENCE |
| BR-TXC-15 | Application-level tax checks (unique name, group country, cash-basis account, distribution set, grouped-tax scope) are not database objects | always | VDR-TXC-C102 VDR-TXC-C097 VDR-TXC-C098 VDR-TXC-C100 | FACT |

## Discovered supporting modules, contradictions, RT list

**Discovered supporting modules (read only as far as needed):** `analytic` (analytic mixin index), `phone_validation` (phone index hook), `mail`, `rating`, `website`/`auth`-class mixins (inherited fields only), `account_check_printing`, `account_qr_code_emv`, `account_edi_ubl_cii`, `hr_expense`, `stock_account`, `sale_stock`/`purchase_stock` (field redeclarations); uninstalled (field declarations only): `account_debit_note`, `account_tax_python`, `account_update_tax_tags`, `base_vat`, `l10n_account_withholding_tax`.

**Contradictions with prior evidence:** none against B01 or U13/U24 on counts and seeded data. Refinement of B01: its statically declared field total (12,223) is 112 below the raw field declarations found here (12,335); two causes traced (annotated declarations in `base`, `report/` models in `hr_skills`), remainder untraced. No DB contradiction.

**RT items (runtime/AWT required):** (1) effective tax group of the four zero/exempt VAT taxes on documents and returns; (2) python tax constraints (name uniqueness, group country, distribution validity, cash-basis account); (3) stored tax/rounding/rate/cutoff columns cannot be observed — no entries; (4) return rendering and tag-sign application; (5) fiscal position auto-selection; (6) override precedence partner/product/fiscal position/company; (7) behaviour of installing uninstalled tax-path packages (new columns/tables); (8) lock-date and lock-exception enforcement; (9) `init()` index creation on module update; (10) constraint error handling.

**Not read / limits (honest partial):** peripheral models (`res.partner`, `res.company`, `product.*`, `sale.*`, `purchase.*`, `stock.move`, `hr.expense`, currency, country) are mapped by a documented filter, not field-by-field in the register (reconciliation did cover every field); behaviour (compute/onchange/constraint bodies) was read only for the cited methods; 121 other uninstalled modules were counted, not read; no statutory source was consulted; no Function-ID matches beyond PCO-F01/PCO-F02.
